# Target blueprint 13: Api Events And Integrations (part 1 of 4)

> **Status.** Archive: proposal, not ratified. A greenfield redesign blueprint written in October 2026. Every area was Draft, Under Review or Revision Required (its own document status) and no test was run against it. Its content was mined into `docs/blueprint/`; `SSOT.md` remains the source of intent. Module federation described here was not adopted: the built system is a modular monolith (`ARCHITECTURE.md`).
>
> **What this file holds.** All files of the former `Yash_Final_Project_Document/13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/` folder, in order.
>
> **Merged on 2026-11-25** from the files listed below; each one is a section under its original title. Nothing was removed; the originals are in git history at commit `08387ec`.

## Action-to-Operation Map (UI actions to API operations)

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/ACTION_TO_OPERATION_MAP.md`._

Doc ID: MAP-01 · Owner: Backend Architecture Team + Interaction Design Lead · Version 1.0 · Status: Under Review · Relates: UI-04 `07_UI_UX_AND_DESIGN_SYSTEM/ACTION_REGISTRY.md`, APIC-01, APIC-02 `API_CATALOG.md#record-api-catalog-addendum`, `contracts/openapi.yaml`, `contracts/openapi.addendum.yaml`, REVW-09, FF-06

Supersession: supersedes nothing. It is an additional, generated map. It does not edit ACTION_REGISTRY: that registry keeps its placeholder names `API-<module>-<verb>` until its owner applies the placeholder table in section 4. Linked from `13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/INDEX.md` by the documentation architect.

All content is **[TARGET]**. Generated by a script (kept outside the repository) that reads ACTION_REGISTRY, API_CATALOG, `openapi.yaml` and `openapi.addendum.yaml`; the script fails on any ACT whose placeholder has no entry. The catalog ids below are final; operationIds are copied from the contract files, never written by hand.

### 1. Status vocabulary and result

| Status     | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| OK         | Every API the action needs has an `operationId` in `openapi.yaml` or `openapi.addendum.yaml`                                                                                                       |
| CATALOGUED | Every API has a catalog row (`API_CATALOG.md` or `API_CATALOG.md#record-api-catalog-addendum`) but at least one has no OpenAPI operation yet (non-P0 domains; due before their work package exits) |
| MISSING    | No operation and no catalog row can serve the action, or the action's semantics are undecided; the reason is in the Note column                                                                    |
| CLIENT     | The action calls no server operation (navigation, local setting, local draft)                                                                                                                      |
| DEFERRED   | The action belongs to a deferred domain (networking, DOM-14, Ph4) and is out of the launch registries                                                                                              |

**CI rule (fail on unresolved):** the generator is the CI check `act-op-map`: it fails when an ACT row cites a placeholder with no mapping, when a mapped id is in neither catalog, or when the count of MISSING rises above the number recorded in this file. Until the check exists in CI, this file is regenerated in the contract review gate.

| Result                                                        | Count |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- |
| ACT rows                                                      | 231   |
| OK                                                            | 153   |
| CATALOGUED                                                    | 59    |
| MISSING                                                       | 0     |
| CLIENT                                                        | 16    |
| DEFERRED                                                      | 3     |
| Actions without an `operationId` today (CATALOGUED + MISSING) | 59    |

**MISSING after this pass: 0.** ACT-HIRE-09 was MISSING; it is reclassified CLIENT on a **PROPOSED** resolution (2026-10-27, awaiting product-owner confirmation): job "Archive" is not a server state. SM-JOB `closed` stays terminal; the default jobs list (`API-jobs-list-org`) excludes `closed`, and the user reaches closed jobs through the existing status filter. No new operation, state or event is introduced. If the owner wants a distinct archived state, that is a new SM-JOB transition and API, to be raised as an OPEN decision.

### 3. Operations added by this pass

`openapi.addendum.yaml` defines **117** operations with full request and response schemas for the P0 domains: 60 new operations (identity, orgs, profile, evidence, verification, jobs, applications, matching/search, messaging, privacy, platform operations) and 57 operations of existing APIC-01 rows that had only reserved schema names (orgs, profile, skills read, search and matching, messaging, notifications). `API_CATALOG.md#record-api-catalog-addendum` adds **43** catalogue-only rows (later phases). Combined catalog: 230 + 60 + 43 = 333 rows; combined contract: 109 + 117 = 226 operations. Operation ids follow the existing convention `<module><Resource><Verb>`.

### 5. Findings the map surfaced (not fixed here)

1. **Evidence archive** (ACT-EVD-06) is served by the withdraw operation API-05-05 after the 8 s client-side delay defined in SM-00-R2 section 2.2 (no archived state exists, no server-side undo). **Job archive** (ACT-HIRE-09) is reclassified CLIENT on a PROPOSED resolution (2026-10-27, owner confirmation needed): SM-JOB `closed` is terminal, SM-00-R2 gives archive semantics for evidence only, so "Archive" hides a closed job from the default list and the status filter reveals it; no operation is needed.
2. **State machines now exist** in `STATE_MACHINES.md#record-state-machines-rev2` for first contact (SM-FIRSTCONTACT), organization verification (SM-ORG), credential, support grant, course, dispute, work claim and export; the addendum operations carry `x-state-machine` for them and use their persisted sets. SM-00-R2 does not give **offers** a machine: API-10-20/21 derive offer status from the application state and the offer's timestamps. Org verification has operations (API-02-13/14) but **no ACT row** submits a verification: the action registry lacks an organization-verification action (HIR-16 screen) and needs one.
3. `API-identity-reactivate` maps to sign-in (API-01-02 / 01-04): SM-ACCOUNT t6 reactivates on sign-in, so no separate operation exists; SM-SESSION t1 requires `account active` and must allow a `deactivated` account to authenticate into reactivation.
4. `API-applications-attach-evidence` and `API-applications-create-draft` both map to the draft PUT (API-10-04): the draft payload must carry the evidence ids and resume choice; confirm the `ApplicationDraftPut.payload` shape in the next contract revision.
5. OFF-01 names `PUT .../drafts/{draftId}`; the catalog route is `PUT /me/application-drafts/{jobId}` (API-10-04). The catalog is taken as authoritative; OFF-01 should cite it.
6. Institution rows moved to the MOD-INS consumer (REVW-08) are listed in `API_CATALOG.md#record-api-catalog-addendum` section 3.

### 6. What was validated (and what was not)

See `API_CATALOG.md#record-api-catalog-addendum` section 6 for the validator runs. This map's own checks: every one of the 231 ACT ids parsed; every placeholder resolved; every cited catalog id exists in a catalog; every operationId was read from a contract file.

## API Catalog (Target)

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/API_CATALOG.md`._

Doc ID: APIC-01 · Owner: Backend Architecture Team · Version 1.0 · Status: Under Review · Relates: ADR-T07, ADR-T08, INV-T01, INV-T02, INV-T10, INV-T15, FF-01, FF-05, FF-06, FF-07 · Companions: API-STD `API_DESIGN_STANDARDS.md`, `contracts/openapi.yaml`, EVC-01, BE-01

> **REV2 folded in (2026-10-27):** rows replaced/added from `API_CATALOG.md#record-api-catalog-addendum`; the addendum text stays for overrides in its sections 3-5.

Tags: **[TARGET]** design; nothing is implemented. This catalog lists **230** endpoints across 24 domains; **109** have full OpenAPI 3.1 path and schema definitions in `contracts/openapi.yaml` (identity, evidence, verification, jobs, applications, billing, privacy, plus the file-upload and capabilities routes they depend on). For the others the request/response schema names are reserved [TARGET] and their schemas are authored when the owning module is specified (tracked as the "OpenAPI" column).

The candidate source mapping for root SSOT `SCI-01…SCI-54` is in [SSOT_SERVICE_CONTRACT_CROSSWALK](13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS_PART2.md#ssot-service-contract-crosswalk) and its [row-level CSV](13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS_PART2.md#ssot_service_contract_crosswalkcsv). It is not an approved API contract or implementation coverage claim; owner disposition remains open under OPEN-46/50.

The [278-row observed route-to-target crosswalk](13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS_PART2.md#observed_route_target_crosswalkcsv) records 9 name-preserving method/path overlaps and 4 same-name-path method mismatches. Among the remaining 265 source routes, 6 have parameter-agnostic path-shape candidates (5 same method, 1 different method) and 259 have no target path-shape match. Parameter-name-insensitive candidates are explicitly separate from direct path matches; no source/target semantic equivalence or implementation coverage is asserted (OPEN-46/50).

All paths are relative to `/api/v1`. Reference-project routes (`API_CONTRACTS.md`) are **[REF]** only and were not copied.

### Legend

| Column        | Values                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| AuthN         | `S` session cookie (+ `X-CSRF-Token` on unsafe methods) · `R` refresh cookie (+CSRF) · `T` single-use capability token in path/body (referee, reset, invitation, unsubscribe; hash-only at rest, INV-T05) · `SIG` provider signature (Stripe) · `O` optional session (public route that personalises) · `N` none · `+SVC` also accepts a ROLE-SVC service token (client credentials, scoped) |
| Policy action | `<module>.<resource>.<verb>` registered in `packages/policy`; every route has one (FF-01). Public/token routes still declare one so the registry is total                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Errors        | HTTP status; `status:type` adds a specific problem type (catalogue in API-STD §5). Every route can additionally return 401/403/429/500/503 as per the standards; only route-specific errors are listed                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Pagination    | `Cursor` = opaque keyset cursor + `limit` (API-STD §6); lists without it are bounded by definition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Idempotency   | `Key` accepts `Idempotency-Key`; `Key (required)` rejects absent key (428); `If-Match` required for optimistic concurrency (428/412); `Natural key` is idempotent by resource identity; `n/a` = read-only or token-consuming (single use is the guard)                                                                                                                                       |
| Rate limit    | Class from API-STD §9 (RL-AUTH, RL-TOKEN, RL-READ, RL-WRITE, RL-HEAVY, RL-PUBLIC, RL-WEBHOOK)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| OpenAPI       | `Y` full path+schemas present in `contracts/openapi.yaml` · `-` schema names reserved                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Coverage checks (mechanical)

- Every row has a policy action (229 distinct actions; some routes share an action when they are two doors to one capability, e.g. `identity.session.create`). FF-01 passes by construction.
- Unsafe methods: 131; of these 84 accept `Idempotency-Key` or are naturally idempotent, 21 are guarded by `If-Match` only, and the remainder are token-consuming, deletions of an already-addressed resource, or logout (idempotent by HTTP semantics; FF-07 lint accepts `idempotent:'http'` for DELETE and for token-consuming routes with a justification in the route registry).
- AuthN mix: N: 11, O: 10, R: 1, S: 192, S+SVC: 8, SIG: 2, T: 6.
- Cross-tenant canary (FF-05) is generated from every row whose path contains `{orgId}`, `{institutionId}` or a resource id loaded by a tenant-owned repository.

### Notes

- Webhook receivers (`POST /webhooks/stripe`) are in the catalog but not callable by the product UI; their security is the signature, not the cookie.
- `GET /healthz` and `GET /readyz` are unversioned operational routes mounted at the root in deployment; they are listed under `/api/v1` here only for completeness of the policy registry.
- Admin routes (`/admin/*`) are served only to remote MOD-ADM and are additionally IP/network-policy restricted at the edge [TARGET]; the authoritative control is policy + grants.
- Endpoints for DOM-14 are **phase 3, conditional** (DOMAIN_ARCHITECTURE §6).
- Search/Match: `GET /search/jobs` and `GET /jobs` are deliberately separate: the former ranks (heavier limit class), the latter filters deterministically (cheap, cacheable).

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--api-catalog-addendum-apic-02"></a>

## API Catalog Addendum (APIC-02)

Doc ID: APIC-02 · Owner: Backend Architecture Team · Version 1.0 · Status: Under Review · Relates: APIC-01 `API_CATALOG.md`, API-STD, MAP-01 `ACTION_TO_OPERATION_MAP.md`, REVW-08, REVW-09, REVW-12, REVW-14, REVW-34

> **Rows folded into `API_CATALOG.md` on 2026-10-27.** Sections 3-5 (overrides) still apply; the OpenAPI addendum is merged into `contracts/openapi.yaml`.

Supersession: supersedes nothing; it adds rows and states overrides. APIC-01 stays as published; where this addendum states an override, readers apply it. All **[TARGET]**; nothing is implemented.

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--1-scope"></a>

### 1. Scope

New rows keep the APIC-01 column legend and numbering (the next free number per domain; no existing id is renumbered). Section 2 lists the **117** operations that have full OpenAPI definitions in `contracts/openapi.addendum.yaml` (OpenAPI = `Y (addendum)`): 60 new rows and 57 existing APIC-01 rows in P0 domains (orgs, profile, skills read, search, matching, messaging, notifications) that previously had only reserved schema names. Section 3 lists **43** catalogue-only rows for later phases (OpenAPI = `-`). Combined: 333 catalog rows, 226 with an OpenAPI operation (109 in `openapi.yaml` + 117 in the addendum).

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--2-operations-defined-in-the-addendum-p0-domains"></a>

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--31-consumer-remote-overrides-revw-08-backend-part"></a>

#### 3.1 Consumer-remote overrides (REVW-08, backend part)

REV-RES splits MOD-ADM into **MOD-ADM** (platform back-office: support, moderation, admin, auditor, flags) and **MOD-INS** (`mf-institution`, `/institution/*`: institution customers, faculty, proctors). Back-office code is served only behind an authenticated edge rule, so customer-facing rows must not name MOD-ADM. Overrides to APIC-01's Consumer column, derived from the screen each action lives on in ACTION_REGISTRY:

| API IDs                                                                                                  | APIC-01 consumer | Consumer now                                        | Reason                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| API-18-01 to API-18-06 and all new API-18-xx                                                             | MOD-ADM          | **MOD-INS**                                         | Institution workspace (ADM-17 to ADM-22 are institution screens) |
| API-02-05 (faculty and proctor invitations in an institution)                                            | MOD-ADM          | MOD-HIR (org) and **MOD-INS** (institution context) | ACT-INS-07                                                       |
| API-02-03, 02-04, 02-07 to 02-11 (organization settings, members, SSO, access review)                    | MOD-ADM          | **MOD-HIR**                                         | Customer org administration lives on HIR-15 to HIR-17 screens    |
| API-23-01 to 23-07, 23-09, 23-10 (webhook endpoints, API clients, imports of an org)                     | MOD-ADM          | **MOD-HIR**                                         | Customer-owned settings; not back-office                         |
| API-06-14, 06-16, 06-18; 07-10, 07-11; 19-02 to 19-04; 20-xx; 17-05, 17-06; 04-05, 04-06; 24-06 to 24-10 | MOD-ADM          | MOD-ADM (unchanged)                                 | Platform back-office                                             |

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--32-deferred-domain-revw-34-api-part"></a>

#### 3.2 Deferred domain (REVW-34, API part)

DOM-14 networking (API-14-01 to API-14-03, paths `/network/*`) is **deferred to Ph4** (ROADMAP WP-COMMUNITY; FEAT-14-01 phase 4). The three rows stay in APIC-01 for history but are **removed from the launch registries**: they get no route in the Ph1 to Ph3 route registry, no OpenAPI path, no policy action in the launch `actions.ts`, and ACT-NET-01 to ACT-NET-03 are marked DEFERRED in MAP-01. The header "phase 3, conditional" in APIC-01 DOM-14 is superseded by "Ph4, deferred". The public developer API (DOM-23) phase is Ph3 (WP-INTEGRATIONS-API); FEAT-23-01/02 "phase 4" is stale.

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--33-problem-type-alignment-revw-19-and-sm-00"></a>

#### 3.3 Problem type alignment (REVW-19 and SM-00)

SM-00 U2 and several acceptance criteria say `409 invalid_transition`; API-STD section 5 and every OpenAPI response say `state-transition-invalid`. **Canonical: `state-transition-invalid`** (kebab-case slug like every other problem type; RFC 9457 `type` is `/problems/state-transition-invalid`). SM-00 U2, SPEC-G2 AC-05-05-05 and SPEC-G4 AC-10-05-03/04 should be amended to the canonical slug; until then they read as the same type. Listed for the governance agent.

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--34-idempotency-declaration-revw-14"></a>

#### 3.4 Idempotency declaration (REVW-14)

FF-07 is reworded to: every unsafe route **accepts `Idempotency-Key` or declares a natural key** (route registry field `idempotent: 'natural'` with the key named, or `idempotent: 'none'` with a stated reason for token-consuming and credential routes); a route flagged `queuedOp: true` additionally needs the 14-day `queued` retention class or a natural key (BACKEND_ARCHITECTURE_REV2 section 4). Checked on the merged contract: **25** unsafe operations of `openapi.yaml` carry no `Idempotency-Key` parameter (the REVW-14 count). Their declarations, to be added as `x-idempotency` by the owner of `openapi.yaml`:

| Declaration                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Operations                                                                                      |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `natural`: set-to-absent or set-to-value; a repeat has the same outcome                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | API-01-06 (logout), 01-08, 01-21, 05-07, 06-10, 09-09, 09-13 (DELETE of a resource by identity) |
| `natural` guarded by `If-Match`: a repeat after success returns 412; the client treats 412 whose current state equals the intended state as success. When the outbox activates (ADR-T10 trigger) every one of these that enters the offline queue must also accept `Idempotency-Key` (lint fails until it does) | API-01-16, 05-04, 05-05, 05-09, 06-04, 09-05, 10-04, 10-14                                      |
| `none`: token-consuming or credential routes, single use or a state check is the guard                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | API-01-02, 01-03, 01-04, 01-05, 01-09, 01-10, 01-11, 01-17, 01-18, 06-06                        |

The addendum operations carry an `x-idempotency` extension stating which of the three applies, and the 26 APIC-01 rows marked `n/a` fall into the same table. Host rule (HC-01): a remote passes one key per user intent and the host never invents a fresh key per call.

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--4-evidence-issuer-class-revw-12-backend-part"></a>

### 4. Evidence issuer class (REVW-12, backend part)

`issuer_class` ∈ {`platform`, `institution`, `employer_org`, `instructor`} is mandatory on every `authority_verified` rung. Contract: `contracts/schemas/EvidenceIssuer.schema.json` and the components `IssuerClass`, `EvidenceIssuer`, `EvidenceItemWithIssuer`, `EvidenceProvenance` in `openapi.addendum.yaml`; operation API-05-15 returns provenance with the issuer; API-05-18 (Ph2) is the instructor sign-off. Database part: PHYSICAL_DESIGN_REV2 section 6. `EvidenceItemWithIssuer` supersedes the `EvidenceItem` response for new clients; merge the optional `issuer` field into `EvidenceItem` in the next contract revision.

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--5-event-envelope-revw-44"></a>

### 5. Event envelope (REVW-44)

`EventEnvelope.actor` gains `human` (boolean, producer-derived). "Human actor" in SM-00 U6 and INV-T09 means `human = true` and `kind ∈ {user, support}`. Changed in place in `EventEnvelope.schema.json`, `asyncapi.yaml` and EVENT_CATALOG section 1 earlier in this pass (see the report).

<a id="record-api-catalog-addendum--6-validators-run-on-the-contracts-this-pass"></a>

### 6. Validators run on the contracts (this pass)

All runs were made from a temporary directory **outside the repository** (installed there: `@redocly/cli` 1.25.15 and `@asyncapi/parser` 3.4.0); nothing was installed into the repository. Because `openapi.addendum.yaml` is a fragment whose `$ref`s point at components of `openapi.yaml`, it is validated **after a merge** that a script performs in the temporary directory (assertions: no operationId, `x-api-id` or schema-name clash; no method clash on a shared path).

| Check                                                                                                                                   | Tool                                                                                                                                                          | Outcome                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| YAML load of `openapi.yaml`, `openapi.addendum.yaml`, `asyncapi.yaml`                                                                   | PyYAML 6.0.3                                                                                                                                                  | all three load                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `openapi.yaml` alone                                                                                                                    | `redocly lint` (built-in `recommended` ruleset, no custom config)                                                                                             | valid, 0 errors, 0 warnings                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `openapi.yaml` + addendum merged (226 operations: 109 + 117)                                                                            | `redocly lint` (same ruleset)                                                                                                                                 | **valid, 0 errors, 1 warning**: `no-ambiguous-paths` between `/notifications/{notificationId}/read-markers` and `/notifications/unsubscribe/{token}` (both are existing APIC-01 rows, API-15-02 and API-15-08, whose paths were not changed here; a request to `/notifications/unsubscribe/read-markers` would match both). Proposed fix for the catalog owner: move the token route to `/unsubscribe/{token}` |
| `asyncapi.yaml` (after the envelope `actor.human` edit)                                                                                 | `@asyncapi/parser` 3.4.0                                                                                                                                      | valid; 1 informational hint (`asyncapi-latest-version`: the document is 3.0.0, the parser suggests 3.1.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| JSON Schema files in `contracts/schemas` (4, including the new `EvidenceIssuer.schema.json` and the edited `EventEnvelope.schema.json`) | `jsonschema` 4.26.0 `Draft202012Validator.check_schema`                                                                                                       | all four are valid 2020-12 schemas                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Enum-vs-SM check (FF-11 style) on the merged contract                                                                                   | script that parses persisted state sets from `STATE_MACHINES.md` and `STATE_MACHINES.md#record-state-machines-rev2` and compares every `x-state-machine` enum | 23 enums matched, 0 mismatches (15 in `openapi.yaml`, 8 added here); `BulkStageTransitionRequest.toState` uses `x-state-machine-subset` because it is a deliberate subset                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Route lint on the merged contract                                                                                                       | script                                                                                                                                                        | every one of the 226 operations has `x-policy-action`; 25 unsafe operations of `openapi.yaml` have no `Idempotency-Key` parameter and no `x-idempotency` (classified in section 3.4); all 117 addendum operations declare one                                                                                                                                                                                  |

**Not run:** `oasdiff` (FF-06) and an AsyncAPI generator or CLI lint (only the parser ran); no SQL was executed (the DDL in `PHYSICAL_DESIGN.md#record-physical-design-rev2` is unparsed and unrun: no PostgreSQL instance was available); `redocly` was not run on the addendum alone because its references need the base file.

## API Design Standards

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/API_DESIGN_STANDARDS.md`._

Doc ID: API-STD · Owner: Backend Architecture Team · Version 1.0 · Status: Under Review · Relates: ADR-T06, ADR-T07, ADR-T08, ADR-T18, INV-T01, INV-T02, INV-T10, INV-T13, INV-T15, FF-01, FF-06, FF-07 · Companions: APIC-01 `API_CATALOG.md`, `contracts/openapi.yaml`, BE-01 §4-§9

Tags: **[TARGET]** decision · **[OPEN]** open · **[REF]** reference. Numeric limits are **[TARGET]** starting values with rationale; calibration is OPEN-B2. The reference project's `API_CONTRACTS.md` is **[REF]** only.

**Root SSOT reconciliation (OPEN-46):** SSOT §§12.18–12.20 (v6.2 numbering) specifies a `{success,data,meta}` success/list envelope, cursor default 20, and a minimum deprecation period of six months. This target standard specifies `{items,page}` list pagination with default 25 and a 90-day deprecation window. RFC 9457 `problem+json` describes errors and may coexist with a success envelope; whether the SSOT success envelope is retained is not yet decided. These contract differences remain open, and this target document does not silently supersede the SSOT.

The candidate row-level mapping for SSOT `CON-001…CON-016` is in [SSOT_INTERFACE_CONTRACT_CROSSWALK](13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS_PART2.md#ssot-interface-contract-crosswalk) and [CSV](13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS_PART2.md#ssot_interface_contract_crosswalkcsv). It records two conflicts and remaining partial mappings; no equivalence is approved.

### 1. Principles

1. The contract is the Zod schema in `packages/contracts`; OpenAPI 3.1, JSON Schema and TypeScript client types are generated from it (ADR-T08). Hand-edited OpenAPI is a defect.
2. Every route declares a policy action; the contract documents it as `x-policy-action` (FF-01). Authorisation is server-side and per resource (INV-T01/T02). A client never learns permissions except as UI hints from `GET /me/capabilities`.
3. Safe by default: unknown request fields are rejected; response fields are additive; unsafe operations are retry-safe (INV-T10); concurrent edits are detected (ETag).
4. The API exposes _use cases_, not tables: state changes are modelled as resources (`/publication`, `/closure`, `/withdrawal`, `/stage-transitions`, `/decision`) so that history, idempotency and policy attach naturally.
5. No cross-origin API for the browser: the shell, remotes and API share one origin through the BFF routing layer; there is no CORS allowlist to misconfigure. Integrators use service tokens (§4) from servers.

### 2. Resource, URL and payload conventions

| Topic                | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Base                 | `/api/v1`; JSON only (`application/json; charset=utf-8`); errors `application/problem+json`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Paths                | lowercase, plural nouns, kebab-case multiword (`saved-searches`, `reference-requests`); ids in path are opaque UUIDs; `me` is the only alias and resolves to the authenticated account; nesting at most two levels (`/orgs/{orgId}/jobs`); a resource addressable by id is also available at its top-level path (`/jobs/{jobId}`) for policy lookup by resource |
| Verbs                | Use HTTP methods for CRUD. State transitions are `POST` to a noun that names the _result_ of the transition (`POST /jobs/{id}/closure`). No verbs in paths except established protocol endpoints (`/auth/login`)                                                                                                                                                |
| Fields               | `camelCase`; timestamps RFC 3339 UTC with `Z`; dates `YYYY-MM-DD`; enums lowercase `snake_case` strings (never integers); money = integer minor units + ISO 4217 code; ids `uuid`; booleans never tri-state (use explicit enum); absent vs `null`: absent = not provided / not visible to caller, `null` = known empty                                          |
| Masking              | Fields the caller may not see (consent, masking obligations) are **omitted**, not nulled, and the representation carries no hint of their existence beyond documented `consentLive`-style flags                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Sizes                | Request body <= 1 MB JSON (uploads go to presigned URLs); string maxima declared per field; arrays have `maxItems`; rejected with 413/422                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Locale               | `Accept-Language` affects only problem `title`/`detail` text and server-rendered strings; data is never translated implicitly                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Methods              | `PUT` replaces a whole sub-resource and requires `If-Match` where versioned; `PATCH` is JSON Merge Patch semantics on documented fields only                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Idempotent semantics | `GET/PUT/DELETE` idempotent by HTTP; `POST` idempotent via `Idempotency-Key` (§8)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Redirects            | Only for protocol flows (SSO). APIs return 303/302 `Location` only on documented routes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### 3. Versioning and evolution (ADR-T08, ADR-T18)

| Rule                                 | Detail                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Major in URL                         | `/api/v1`. A `v2` exists only for unavoidable breaking change; v1 and v2 run side by side for a documented window                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Compatible changes (no version bump) | Add optional request field, add response field, add enum value **only where the contract states "clients must tolerate unknown enum values"** (all response enums carry this note), add endpoint, loosen validation, add problem type                                                                           |
| Breaking changes                     | Remove/rename field, tighten validation, change type/semantics, remove enum value, change auth or idempotency semantics, change default sort. Detected by `oasdiff` in CI (FF-06); a breaking diff on v1 fails the build                                                                                        |
| Remote compatibility                 | Each remote manifest declares `apiRange`; the shell refuses to activate a remote outside the range and falls back to last-known-good (ADR-T18). Because the PWA may run an old shell/remote for days (service-worker cache), the API supports the previous _remote_ release for at least one deprecation window |
| Deprecation                          | `Deprecation: true` + `Sunset: <date>` + `Link: <doc>; rel="deprecation"` headers; minimum window 90 days [TARGET: remotes release independently and installed PWAs update lazily; shorter windows strand clients]; usage telemetry decides removal                                                             |
| Experimental                         | Routes tagged `x-stability: experimental` may change without notice; none are exposed to the product UI                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### 4. Authentication and request provenance (ADR-T06)

| Caller                        | Mechanism                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Browser (shell + all remotes) | `__Host-ts_at` (15 min; `HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax; Path=/`) and `__Secure-ts_rt` (refresh; `HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict; Path=/api/v1/auth/refresh` — the `__Host-` prefix cannot be path-scoped) cookies. Remotes call `fetch('/api/v1/...')` through the shell's fetch wrapper with `credentials: 'same-origin'`; they never read tokens (INV-T16)                                                                                                                |
| CSRF                          | Unsafe methods require `X-CSRF-Token` (double-submit value issued by `GET /me` / `POST /auth/login`) **and** pass an `Origin`/`Sec-Fetch-Site: same-origin` check. `SameSite=Lax` is defence in depth, not the control                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Step-up                       | Operations marked `step-up-required` (password change, erasure request, data export, passkey removal, SSO config) need `recentAuthAt` within 10 minutes [TARGET] or return `403 step-up-required` with the acceptable `amr` methods; the client re-authenticates then retries with the same Idempotency-Key                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Capability tokens             | Referee, password-reset, email-verify, org-invitation, unsubscribe tokens: >=256-bit random, single use, expiring, bound to one request, stored hash-only (INV-T05); sent in the path/body, never in query strings (avoids referer/log leakage); responses set `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, `Cache-Control: no-store`; invalid/expired/consumed tokens return an indistinguishable `404` (expired may return `410` only where the token is still bound to a visible request) |
| Service tokens                | ROLE-SVC clients (`api_client`) obtain short-lived (<=1 h) tokens by client-credentials from `POST /oauth/token` [TARGET; route in APIC-01, details OPEN-B16]; scopes are a subset of the owning org's role; accepted only on routes whose catalog authN includes `+SVC`; every call attributed in `audit_event`                                                                                                                                                                |
| Provider callbacks            | Signature verification over the raw body with timestamp tolerance (Stripe: 5 min) before any parsing; replay protection by event id (INV-T17)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Trace                         | `traceparent` accepted and propagated; `X-Request-Id` echoed; both appear in every problem body as `traceId`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### 5. Error model — RFC 9457 problem+json (ADR-T08)

Body: `{type, title, status, detail?, instance?, code, traceId, retryAfterSeconds?}`; validation errors add `errors: [{pointer, code, message}]` (JSON Pointer into the request). `type` is a stable URI `https://errors.talentsphere.example/<slug>` (placeholder authority; resolved to docs) and `code` repeats the slug for programmatic use. Messages never include secrets, SQL, stack traces, other users' data, or whether a _different_ user's resource exists.

| Slug (`code`)              | Status | When                                                     | Client action              | Retry             |
| -------------------------- | ------ | -------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ----------------- |
| `malformed-request`        | 400    | Unparseable JSON, bad cursor, bad header                 | Fix request                | No                |
| `unauthenticated`          | 401    | No/expired/invalid session or token                      | Refresh then re-login      | After refresh     |
| `session-reuse-detected`   | 401    | Rotated refresh token replayed; family revoked (BR-T050) | Full re-login              | No                |
| `entitlement-required`     | 402    | Plan/quota does not permit the action                    | Show upgrade path          | After plan change |
| `forbidden`                | 403    | Authenticated but policy denies                          | Hide/disable               | No                |
| `consent-required`         | 403    | Needed consent scope absent/revoked (INV-T15)            | Explain; request consent   | After consent     |
| `ai-prohibited`            | 403    | AI blocked by assessment mode (INV-T06)                  | Explain                    | No                |
| `step-up-required`         | 403    | Recent authentication needed                             | Re-authenticate            | Yes               |
| `grant-required`           | 403    | Support access needs live grant (BR-T041)                | Request grant              | After grant       |
| `not-found`                | 404    | Missing **or not visible** to caller                     | -                          | No                |
| `conflict`                 | 409    | Uniqueness/state conflict (generic)                      | Refetch                    | Maybe             |
| `duplicate-application`    | 409    | BR-T001                                                  | Show existing application  | No                |
| `state-transition-invalid` | 409    | Not allowed by SM-* (INV-T11)                            | Refetch state              | No                |
| `reminder-limit-reached`   | 409    | BR-T004 (max 2)                                          | -                          | No                |
| `idempotency-in-flight`    | 409    | Same key running                                         | Wait `Retry-After`         | Yes               |
| `gone`                     | 410    | Expired token/request/export                             | Re-request                 | No                |
| `precondition-failed`      | 412    | `If-Match` stale                                         | Refetch, merge, retry      | After refetch     |
| `payload-too-large`        | 413    | Body/file over cap                                       | Reduce                     | No                |
| `unsupported-media-type`   | 415    | Content-type not allowlisted                             | Fix                        | No                |
| `validation-failed`        | 422    | Schema/business validation                               | Fix fields (`errors[]`)    | No                |
| `idempotency-key-reuse`    | 422    | Key reused with different request                        | New key                    | No                |
| `account-locked`           | 423    | Throttled/locked login                                   | Wait / recover             | After `until`     |
| `precondition-required`    | 428    | Missing `If-Match` or required `Idempotency-Key`         | Add header                 | Yes               |
| `rate-limited`             | 429    | Class limit exceeded                                     | Back off per `Retry-After` | Yes               |
| `internal`                 | 500    | Unexpected                                               | Report `traceId`           | Idempotent only   |
| `upstream-unavailable`     | 503    | Dependency/breaker open (BE-01 §19)                      | Retry later                | Yes               |
| `upstream-timeout`         | 504    | Dependency timeout                                       | Retry later                | Yes (idempotent)  |

Status-code discipline: `401` never reveals whether an account exists; login failures are uniform; `403` vs `404` follows BE-01 §5 (existence not disclosed). The catalog's "Errors" column lists route-specific statuses only; generic ones apply everywhere.

### 6. Pagination (mandatory for list routes)

| Rule        | Detail                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Style       | Keyset (cursor) pagination. Request: `?limit=<1..100, default 25>&cursor=<opaque>`. Response: `{items: [...], page: {nextCursor, hasMore, limit}}`                                                                         |
| Cursor      | Opaque, HMAC-signed, includes sort key tuple + filter hash + issued-at (valid 24 h); a cursor from a different filter set or principal is rejected (`malformed-request`). Never contains ids of rows the caller cannot see |
| Ordering    | Every list has a total, documented, stable order ending in `id` as tiebreaker (e.g. `createdAt desc, id desc`); ordering parameter `sort` accepts a per-route allowlist                                                    |
| Totals      | No total counts by default (expensive and a side channel). Where the UI needs a number, a separate cheap count endpoint or `page.estimatedTotal` bounded by a cap is provided per route                                    |
| Consistency | Cursor pages are stable under inserts (no offset drift); deletions may shorten a page. Search results use `(rank_bucket, id)` keyset and are best-effort stable                                                            |
| Guard       | A list route without `pagination: cursor` fails the route-registry lint unless its result set is bounded by construction (documented)                                                                                      |

### 7. Filtering, sorting, search

Filters are explicit query parameters named after fields (`state=published&workMode=remote`); multi-values repeat the parameter; ranges use `from`/`to` or `<field>Since`; free text is `q` (length-capped, never concatenated into SQL). Unknown filters -> 422 (no silent ignore). Sorting is an allowlist (`sort=-publishedAt`). No client-supplied field selection or `expand` (keeps policy/masking evaluation per representation simple). Search endpoints (`/search/*`) are separate from list endpoints (§APIC-01 note).

### 8. Idempotency (INV-T10, FF-07)

Header: `Idempotency-Key: <16-64 char client-generated id>`. Semantics per BE-01 §8: same principal + key + identical request returns the stored response with `Idempotency-Replayed: true`; different request body/route -> `422 idempotency-key-reuse`; concurrent duplicate -> `409 idempotency-in-flight` + `Retry-After`; keys expire after 24 h (7 days for payment-adjacent routes). Operations whose absence of a key would risk money, legal effect, or irreversible harm **require** the key (`428` if absent) — listed in the catalog as "Key (required)". Replays of responses larger than 64 KB return `200` with the current representation and the original `Location`. Offline-queued writes (ADR-T10) reuse the key they were enqueued with.

### 9. Rate limiting (BE-01 §16)

| Class      | Applies to                                                 | Initial limit [TARGET]                                                                          | Key                    | Rationale                                                                                                               |
| ---------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RL-AUTH    | login, register, forgot, passkey options, email challenges | 20 / 10 min per IP; 10 / 15 min per account identifier; progressive delay then `account-locked` | IP prefix + identifier | Far above human typo rate, below credential-stuffing economics; separate per-account counter defeats IP rotation        |
| RL-TOKEN   | capability-token routes                                    | 30 / 10 min per IP; 10 / hour per token hash                                                    | IP, token hash         | Token space is 256-bit so guessing is infeasible; limit contains DoS and log-noise                                      |
| RL-READ    | authenticated reads                                        | 600 / min per principal                                                                         | principal              | ~10 req/s sustained is above interactive UI behaviour (bursts of single digits) yet stops bulk scraping through the API |
| RL-WRITE   | authenticated writes                                       | 120 / min per principal                                                                         | principal              | Human editing pace; protects outbox/audit write amplification                                                           |
| RL-HEAVY   | search, recommendations, AI, exports, aggregates           | 30 / min and 300 / hour per principal; per-org 600 / hour                                       | principal, org         | Costly queries/compute; also limits candidate-scraping rate                                                             |
| RL-PUBLIC  | anonymous reads                                            | 120 / min per IP prefix                                                                         | IP                     | CDN absorbs cacheable traffic; limit targets scrapers                                                                   |
| RL-WEBHOOK | provider webhooks                                          | no per-IP quota; signature check first; max 50 concurrent                                       | provider               | Provider IPs change; authenticity is the gate                                                                           |

Responses carry `RateLimit-Policy` / `RateLimit` (IETF draft structured headers) and, on `429`, `Retry-After`. Business quotas (outreach, postings, AI spend) are entitlements surfaced as `402 entitlement-required` / `429` with `quota` details, not rate-limit classes. All limits are config; changes need no deploy. Values are starting points, not benchmarks (OPEN-B2).

### 10. Concurrency and caching

| Topic          | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Strong ETag    | Every versioned resource response carries `ETag`; `GET` with `If-None-Match` returns `304`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `If-Match`     | Required on PATCH/PUT/state-transition routes flagged `m` (catalog "If-Match"); `428` missing, `412` stale (body includes current `ETag` header)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Cache-Control  | Personal/authenticated: `private, no-store` for sensitive classes (referee, privacy, billing, verification), otherwise `private, max-age=0, must-revalidate`; public catalogs (skills, plans, public jobs/profiles): `public, max-age=60, stale-while-revalidate=300` [TARGET] with `Vary: Accept-Language, Cookie` where personalised variants exist (`O` routes avoid shared caching when a session cookie is present) |
| Service worker | Caches only GETs of owner data allowed by the platform matrix (INV-T13); never caches responses with `no-store`, referee/token routes, or any non-GET; cached entries are labelled stale in UI; logout clears caches                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### 11. Long-running and asynchronous operations

`202 Accepted` + a resource representing the work (`ExportJob`, `DataExport`, `ImportJob`, `FileObject`, `Attempt` with `state`) at a stable URL; clients poll with ETag (`Retry-After` hints) or receive a notification. Status vocabulary is explicit per resource (`requested|building|ready|failed|expired`). No custom `/jobs/{id}` status route for product features; operators use `/admin/jobs`. Results needing authorisation (downloads) return short-lived signed URLs via a dedicated `download-url` route after a policy check, never a public object URL.

### 12. Response filtering by consent and role

Serialisation runs after `policy.can` obligations: `field_mask` removes fields; `require_consent` is resolved before the handler returns data. Recruiter-facing evidence representations include `rung`, provenance summary and only consented artifacts. Representations never include `rung` as a settable field in any request schema (INV-T04) and never include AI-origin text labelled as verified evidence (INV-T08).

### 13. Webhooks and callbacks (inbound/outbound)

Inbound provider webhooks: raw-body signature verification, dedupe by provider event id, respond `2xx` fast, process async. Outbound customer webhooks: versioned envelope, HMAC signature (`TS-Signature: t=<ts>,v1=<hmac>`), at-least-once delivery with retries; payload DTOs are separate from internal events (INT-01 §7).

### 14. Contract governance and testing

| Gate                      | Tool / rule                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Generation                | Zod -> OpenAPI 3.1 + JSON Schema; CI fails if the generated file differs from the committed contract                                            |
| Breaking-change detection | `oasdiff` (FF-06); AsyncAPI diff for events                                                                                                     |
| Route registry lint       | Policy action present (FF-01), idempotency declared for unsafe routes (FF-07), pagination for lists, rate class present, problem types declared |
| Contract tests            | Provider tests per route (schema conformance incl. error bodies); consumer checks per remote against its declared `apiRange`                    |
| Security tests            | Generated cross-tenant canary (FF-05), IDOR matrix from the permission matrix, token single-use tests, rate-limit tests                         |
| Docs                      | Catalog (APIC-01) is regenerated from the registry; hand-written prose lives only in this document                                              |

### 15. Open items

| ID       | Item                                                                                                                         |
| -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| OPEN-B16 | Exact `/oauth/token` service-token flow and scope names (to be specified with INT-01 developer platform; DOM-23 conditional) |
| OPEN-B17 | Whether to expose `estimatedTotal` for any list; default is none                                                             |
| OPEN-B18 | Deprecation window length (90 days proposed) vs enterprise contract needs                                                    |

### PATCH 2026-10-27: concurrency and idempotency confirmation (gap check)

Confirmed already documented, no change needed: ETag/`If-Match` (sections above, error rows `precondition-failed` 412 and `precondition-required` 428), `Idempotency-Key` on unsafe POST (section 8; 422 on reuse with different body), and **409** `idempotency-in-flight`. Added clarifications (PROPOSED): (a) `409` means state conflict or in-flight key and is retried only after `Retry-After`/refetch; stale `If-Match` is `412`, not `409`; (b) every mutable resource GET returns a strong `ETag` and the client echoes it in `If-Match`; (c) lost-response outcome discovery via `GET /idempotency/{key}` is specified in `12_DATA_AND_DATABASE/PHYSICAL_DESIGN.md` DC-05 and is not yet in any OpenAPI file.
Spec validation: **validation not run** for `contracts/openapi.yaml`, `contracts/openapi.addendum.yaml` and `contracts/asyncapi.yaml` in this patch (no validator executed); do not read this document as evidence they validate.

## Event Catalog

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/EVENT_CATALOG.md`._

Doc ID: EVC-01 · Owner: Backend Architecture Team · Version 1.0 · Status: Under Review · Relates: ADR-T05, ADR-T09, INV-T10, INV-T12, FF-06, DOM-00 §3 · Machine-readable: `contracts/asyncapi.yaml`, `contracts/schemas/EventEnvelope.schema.json` · Companions: BE-01 §6, API-CAT, INT-01

Tags: **[TARGET]** design. Nothing here is implemented. 74 events; 15 flagged **core** (full operation pairs in AsyncAPI). Event names extend the examples in DOMAIN_ARCHITECTURE §3 (`account.registered`, `evidence.created/verified/revoked`, `application.submitted`, `application.stage.changed`, `job.published/closed`, `consent.changed`, `erasure.completed`, `entitlement.changed`, ...); additions are marked in the notes column of §5.

### 1. Envelope (all events)

| Field                            | Type                         | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| -------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `event_id`                       | UUIDv7                       | Unique id = dedupe key                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `type`                           | string                       | Dotted lowercase name; unit of subscription                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `version`                        | int                          | Payload schema version for this type                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `occurred_at`                    | RFC 3339                     | Business time (transaction time)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `trace_id`                       | 32 hex                       | W3C trace id, restored by worker (ADR-T16)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `tenant`                         | `{org_id?, institution_id?}` | Tenancy scope; consumers must not widen it (INV-T03)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `aggregate`                      | `{type, id, seq}`            | Ordering key and per-aggregate sequence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `actor`                          | `{kind, human, account_id?}` | Who caused it. `kind` ∈ user/service/system/support/referee; `human` is derived by the producer (true for user, support, referee; false for service, system). "Human actor" in SM-00 U6 and INV-T09 means `human = true` and `kind ∈ {user, support}`; a referee is human but never an authorised decider (REVW-44) |
| `correlation_id`, `causation_id` | string / uuid                | Causal chain                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `data`                           | object                       | Type-specific payload (ids and facts; no free-text personal data; consumers fetch details through owner interfaces under `policy.can`)                                                                                                                                                                              |

### 2. Delivery classes (apply to all events unless the row says otherwise)

| Class   | Semantics                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| EC-STD  | At-least-once. Dedupe: `consumer_inbox(consumer, event_id)`. Retry: exponential backoff with full jitter, base 5 s, cap 15 min, 8 attempts, then `dead` (BE-01 §7.3) and alert. Consumers must be idempotent and must tolerate replay of any event within the 14-day outbox retention |
| EC-ORD  | EC-STD plus per-aggregate order handling: consumer tracks `last_seq(aggregate)`; stale (`seq <= last`) skipped; gap (`seq > last+1`) deferred with bounded retries then rebuilt from the owner's interface. Projection tables are rebuildable                                         |
| EC-CRIT | EC-STD with 12 attempts and a page (not just a ticket) on dead-letter, because failure leaves a user-visible or legal obligation unmet (erasure, evidence revocation, verification outcome, billing/entitlement, passed assessment)                                                   |

Failure semantics common to all: a producer never waits for consumers; consumer failure never rolls back the producer; no consumer may be the _only_ enforcement point of a security rule (enforcement is at the source at use time; events drive projections and notifications). Replay/backfill: `POST /admin/jobs/{id}/retry` re-runs a dead consumer job; full projection rebuilds are job kinds owned by each consumer module.

Versioning: additive field = same version; removed/retyped/renamed field = new `version`, producer emits both versions for one deprecation window; consumers declare accepted versions; changes are checked in CI with the AsyncAPI diff (FF-06 extended to events).

### 3. Ordering and dedupe summary

Ordering key = `aggregate.id` of the producer's aggregate (see column). Cross-aggregate order is never assumed; consumers needing a relation (e.g. `evidence.verified` before `application.submitted` references it) re-read state rather than infer from event order. Dedupe is by `event_id` in the consumer's own inbox, in the same transaction as its state change.
